S54 arch-select: static fusion reports (benches/gpu/fusion_report.py; EMIT_FLAGS for an OFF arm)

1. The integrated build (s54/arch-select = s54-base + epilogue, devmap, streams, rowfold,
   retain-resident, arena, l2-raster, arch-select), every probe, five parts, both faces,
   n = 512 / 1024 / 2048 / 4096. Before = S54's placement rungs off
   (--gpu-off=epilogue,devmap,cell-law,rowfold,rowfold-layout,streams,pinned,pdl,arena,
   retain-resident,delta-h2d,lane-stride,l2-window: the s54-base placement); after = the shipped
   per-part table. Summary first, the full tables in part 3.

2. The arch-select rung alone (s54-base table, before the merge): ON vs `--gpu-off=arch-select`
   (every rung ON) changes no placement on any probe, part, face or size; only decode_1024_256
   differs (the table's `retain-delta` OFF row keeps `resident` on the loop). Section 2 below.

=== 2. The arch-select rung alone (s54-base table)

after = the shipped table. benches/gpu/fusion_report.py (EMIT_FLAGS for the OFF arm), HEAD 3aca06d.

Summary: the table changes no placement on any probe, part, face or size (launches, gens, h2d,
d2h, host loops identical). The one program whose counts differ is decode_1024_256, on every part:
the table's `retain-delta` OFF row keeps `resident` on the decode loop (2 tile + 2 gen kernels,
3 launches, 2 h2d / 2 d2h per step); with the table off, `retain-delta` takes the loop (3 tile
kernels, 5 launches, 10 h2d / 5 d2h per step: S52 round trips plus the restricted row site).
Each block below: OFF first when it differs, then ON.

## rtx-3090 exact n=2048: COUNTS DIFFER ON vs OFF
S54 static fusion report — emit --rewrite --target=rtx-3090 --gpu-off=arch-select (face exact), HEAD 3aca06d
